# DisplayNames data ICU4X lacks (cldr-localenames-full localeDisplayNames.json, languages.json,
# territories.json, scripts.json and variants.json; cldr-dates-full dateFields.json): per
# locale 'locale <tag> <parent>', then '<key>\t<value>' for what differs from the parent
# chain; a key is read from the nearest locale of the chain that has it. Keys:
# ca.<calendar> the calendar names (BCP 47 ids); f.<code>.<long|short|narrow> the date-time
# field names (ECMA-402 codes); lp and ls localeDisplayPattern's localePattern and
# localeSeparator; eq.<kind>.<long|short>.<code>, with an empty value, a name equal to its
# code (kind language, dialect, region, script or variant), which applies only where ICU4X
# has no name for the code. Codes are CLDR's keys: variants are uppercase (PINYIN), so
# a reader uppercases a variant subtag before it looks one up.
# Generated by update-cldr-lists.py from CLDR release-48-2.
